Method: 本文提出了UniVVT框架。核心方法包括：1. 一个基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的场景任务感知器，将源视频、目标服装和任务指令联合编码为紧凑的任务感知隐式标记，隐式捕获“转移什么、在哪里转移以及如何转移”的信息。2. 一个轻量级语义桥接模块，将这些标记与扩散式视频生成器的条件空间对齐，以实现连贯的服装转换。3. 一个三阶段渐进训练策略（语义对齐、联合任务适应、灵活分辨率优化），以稳健地耦合异构组件。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，UniVVT实现了最先进的性能，验证了其方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了隐式语义指导可以作为一种简单而有效的替代方案，取代脆弱的几何预处理，实现端到端的虚拟试穿。UniVVT框架简化了部署流程，提高了鲁棒性，并为视频虚拟任务生成了高质量的结果。

Abstract: Video Virtual Try-On (VVT) synthesizes a video of a person wearing a target garment while preserving identity, motion, and scene dynamics. Dominant approaches cast VVT as mask-conditioned video inpainting and rely on separate modules for human parsing, pose estimation, and garment warping. This multi-stage design complicates deployment and, more critically, allows errors in explicit geometric priors to propagate irreversibly into the generated video. We present UniVVT, a unified end-to-end framework that reframes VVT as semantically conditioned video generation, eliminating mask, pose, and warping modules at inference. At its core, a scene-task perceiver built on a Multimodal Large Language Model jointly encodes the source video, target garment, and task instruction into compact, task-aware latent tokens, implicitly capturing what to transfer and where and how to transfer it. A lightweight semantic bridge then aligns these tokens with the conditioning space of a diffusion-based video generator, enabling coherent garment transfer. To robustly couple the heterogeneous components, we devise a three-stage progressive training strategy comprising semantic alignment, joint task adaptation, and flexible-resolution refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UniVVT achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, validating implicit semantic guidance as a simple and effective alternative to fragile geometric preprocessing for end-to-end virtual try-on.

</details>


### [51] [GST-Bench: Can VLMs Develop Global Spatial Awareness from Video?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05747)
*Qifeng Zhang,Kaixiang Huang,Heng Dong,Huang Fang,Junting Chen,Junjie Zhu,Yonghang Chen,Zhiyu Zhang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GST-Bench基准测试，用于评估视频理解中的全局空间智能，发现当前视觉语言模型与人类在该任务上存在显著差距，模型难以从长时程观察中构建全局一致的场景表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注单视角或少视角的局部空间感知，忽略了对连续、长时程视觉流的全局空间意识评估，而这对于具身智能体的空间智能至关重要。因此，需要新的基准来评估和推动全局空间智能的发展。

Method: 1. 构建了GST-Bench基准测试，包含从6790分钟合成生成视频中提取的人工验证问题，要求模型从新视角进行空间推理并映射到全局俯视图。
2. 对22个最先进的视觉语言模型进行了全面的零样本评估。
3. 构建了GST-Bench-Local以探究模型在局部空间理解与全局整合之间的差距。
4. 提供了GST-Train数据集作为补充资源，用于训练全局空间推理。

Result: 评估显示，当前模型与人类表现差距巨大：最强的零样本模型仅获得42.68分，而人类得分为79.08分。通过GST-Bench-Local发现，模型虽具备较强的局部空间理解能力，但无法将长时程观察整合为全局一致的场景表示。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在从长时程视觉流中构建全局空间表征方面存在根本性缺陷。GST-Bench基准和GST-Train数据集的提出，为评估和改进模型的全局空间智能提供了重要工具和研究方向。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is fundamental to embodied agents, yet existing benchmarks focus on local spatial perception from single or few viewpoints, overlooking global spatial awareness over continuous, long-horizon visual streams. To address this limitation, we introduce the Global-Spatial-Temporal Benchmark (GST-Bench), a VQA benchmark for global spatial intelligence in video understanding, comprising human-verified questions derived from 6,790 minutes of synthetically generated video. It requires models to perform accurate spatial inference from novel viewpoints unseen in the input video and to map egocentric observations onto global top-down images. A comprehensive evaluation of 22 state-of-the-art VLMs exposes a striking gap between models and humans: the strongest zero-shot model attains only 42.68, far below the human score of 79.08. To probe the cause of this gap, we construct GST-Bench-Local and find that models, despite strong local spatial understanding under the same task formulation, still fail to consolidate long-horizon observations into a globally consistent scene representation. We further provide GST-Train, a dataset for global spatial reasoning, as a complementary resource to facilitate future research on this challenge.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Relevance: Bayesian Evidence Acquisition for Agentic Whole-Slide Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05757)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了BEACON，一个无需训练、即插即用的智能体框架，用于全切片图像推理。它将证据获取过程重构为贝叶斯概率推断问题，通过顺序选取能最大程度降低诊断不确定性的图像区块，从而更高效、更准确地回答诊断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将全切片图像推理视为基于语义相关性的迭代式区块检索。然而，在计算病理学中，语义相关性并不等同于诊断信息量，因为竞争性诊断常表现出相似且重叠的形态模式，导致许多区块语义相关但无法用于鉴别诊断。这造成了冗余的证据获取，并可能无法解决诊断不确定性。

Method: BEACON将全切片图像推理构建为贝叶斯证据获取问题。它维护一个关于竞争诊断假设的概率信念，并通过最大化期望信息增益来顺序获取图像区块，以减少诊断不确定性。一个证据控制器随后决定是回答问题、获取更多证据，还是进行更高分辨率的检查。该框架完全由现成的视觉语言基础模型构建，无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在五个全切片图像视觉问答基准测试上的广泛零样本实验表明，BEACON在所有无需训练的智能体框架中取得了最强的整体性能，同时显著提高了证据获取效率。

Conclusion: BEACON成功地将贝叶斯证据获取确立为一种有原则、能感知不确定性的智能体全切片图像推理范式。它证明了通过主动获取最具诊断价值的证据，可以超越基于相关性的检索，实现更高效、更准确的零样本病理诊断推理。

Abstract: Whole-slide image (WSI) reasoning requires an agent to sequentially acquire visual evidence before answering a diagnostic question. Existing training-free agentic frameworks formulate this process as iterative patch retrieval based on semantic relevance to the question. However, semantic relevance does not necessarily imply diagnostic informativeness in computational pathology, where competing diagnoses often exhibit similar and overlapping morphological patterns, making many patches semantically relevant yet diagnostically non-discriminative. Consequently, relevance-based retrieval may acquire redundant observations and leave diagnostic uncertainty unresolved. We propose BEACON, a plug-and-play agentic framework that reformulates WSI reasoning as a Bayesian evidence acquisition problem. BEACON maintains a probabilistic belief over competing diagnostic hypotheses and sequentially acquires patches by maximizing expected information gain (EIG) to reduce diagnostic uncertainty. An evidence controller then determines whether to answer, acquire additional evidence, or perform higher-resolution inspection. Built entirely from off-the-shelf foundation models, BEACON requires no additional training or fine-tuning. Extensive zero-shot experiments across five WSI-VQA benchmarks demonstrate that BEACON achieves the strongest overall performance among training-free agentic frameworks while substantially improving evidence acquisition efficiency, establishing Bayesian evidence acquisition as a principled paradigm for uncertainty-aware agentic WSI reasoning. The code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BEACON

</details>


### [53] [Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05769)
*Zihao He,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds (FoRM) 方法，用于图像恢复任务中的知识蒸馏。该方法将基于关系的知识迁移重新定义为关系流形上的连续流映射问题，通过学习一个能预测任意目标时间关系状态的流映射算子，实现了更有效的轨迹级监督，并在多个图像恢复任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图像恢复知识蒸馏方法通常将中间特征或关系矩阵作为静态目标进行对齐，忽略了知识迁移过程的动态结构。本文旨在解决这一局限性，提出一种能够捕捉和利用知识迁移动态特性的新方法。

Method: FoRM的核心是学习一个流映射算子 $\mathcal{F}_θ(\mathbf{z}, t, s)$，它能根据当前时间 $t$ 的状态直接预测目标时间 $s$ 的关系状态，从而实现更丰富的轨迹级监督。为确保流映射的全局自一致性，引入了安全半群一致性约束，利用真实桥接状态强制组合一致性，消除幻影状态误差累积。此外，还设计了端点锚定损失，以防止算子偏离教师目标。

Result: 在五个图像恢复任务（超分辨率、去雨、去噪、去模糊和低光增强）上的大量实验表明，FoRM在多种骨干网络架构上均优于现有的知识蒸馏基线。与朴素的流匹配蒸馏相比，该方法将训练方差降低了约50%，并实现了更优的恢复质量。

Conclusion: FoRM通过将知识迁移建模为关系流形上的连续流映射问题，为图像恢复领域的知识蒸馏提供了一个有效且通用的新框架。其引入的流映射算子和相关约束机制能够显著提升蒸馏效果，降低训练不稳定性，并在多个任务和架构上展现出优越的性能。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation for image restoration typically aligns intermediate features or relation matrices between teacher and student networks as static targets, ignoring the dynamic structure of the knowledge transfer process. In this paper, we propose Flow-Map Distillation on Relation Manifolds (FoRM), which reformulates relation-based knowledge transfer as a continuous flow mapping problem on the relation manifold. Rather than regressing a constant velocity field between student and teacher relation states, FoRM learns a flow map operator $\mathcal{F}_θ(\mathbf{z}, t, s)$ that directly predicts the relation state at any target time $s$ given the current state at time $t$, enabling richer trajectory-level supervision. To ensure global self-consistency of the learned flow map, we introduce a safe semigroup consistency constraint that enforces compositional agreement using ground-truth bridge states, eliminating phantom-state error accumulation. An endpoint anchoring loss further prevents the operator from drifting away from the teacher target. Extensive experiments on five image restoration tasks, including super-resolution, deraining, denoising, deblurring, and low-light enhancement, demonstrate consistent gains over state-of-the-art distillation baselines across multiple backbone architectures, reducing training variance by approximately 50\% compared to naive flow matching distillation while achieving superior restoration quality.

</details>


### [54] [HyTBE: Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert Model for Cross-Domain Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05771)
*Aohua Li,Jin Kuang,Yubing Lu,Pingping Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对红外小目标检测（IRSTD）在跨域场景下性能退化的问题，本文提出了一种双曲目标-背景专家模型（HyTBE），通过目标-背景关系干预扩展训练数据中的关系模式，并利用双曲空间建模与专家混合机制来提升模型对未见域的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRSTD方法主要通过增强目标响应和抑制背景干扰来提升检测性能，但其在有限源域上训练后，学到的决策规则受限于源域中特定的目标-背景关系模式。当遇到未见域时，这些关系模式可能发生偏移，导致模型判别能力下降。本文将这一跨域失败问题归结为“目标-背景关系偏移”。

Method: 提出HyTBE模型，其核心组件包括：1. **目标-背景关系干预**：在训练时对目标或背景进行选择性扰动，以扩展可观测到的关系模式，同时保持有效的监督信号。2. **双曲关系建模**：将多尺度视觉特征映射到庞加莱球中，通过特征与目标/背景锚点之间的相对距离来刻画每个特征的目标-背景关系。3. **双曲引导的MoE适配器**：利用双曲关系表征来校准多尺度视觉特征，并聚合针对不同关系模式的专家特定特征修正。

Result: 在NUAA-SIRST、NUDT-SIRST和IRSTD-1K数据集上进行的留一域实验表明，与现有的竞争性基线方法相比，HyTBE具有更强的跨域泛化性能。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了IRSTD跨域性能下降的根本原因在于目标-背景关系模式的偏移。所提出的HyTBE模型通过创新的干预策略扩展关系模式，并借助双曲几何进行精细的关系建模与特征适配，有效提升了模型对未见红外域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection (IRSTD) has achieved substantial progress under domain-consistent evaluation, yet detector performance often degrades markedly when generalizing to unseen infrared domains. Existing methods primarily improve detection by enhancing target responses and suppressing background interference. However, when trained on only a limited set of source domains, their learned decision rules are inevitably established from a restricted range of source-domain target-background relation patterns. We formulate this cross-domain failure as target-background relation shift: unseen domains may exhibit relation patterns that are not observed during training, thereby weakening the discriminative capability learned from the source domains. To address this problem, we propose HyTBE, a Hyperbolic Target-Background Expert model that expands source-domain relation patterns and adaptively adjusts visual representations using explicit relation cues. The Target-Background Relation Intervention selectively perturbs either targets or backgrounds, broadening the observable relation patterns during training while maintaining valid supervision. Subsequently, the Hyperbolic Relation Modeling maps multi-scale visual cues into a Poincaré ball and characterizes the target-background relation of each feature token according to its relative distances to the target and background anchors. The Hyperbolic-guided MoE Adapter further uses these hyperbolic relation representations to calibrate multi-scale visual features and aggregate expert-specific feature corrections for different relation patterns. Leave-one-domain-out experiments on NUAA-SIRST, NUDT-SIRST, and IRSTD-1K demonstrate that HyTBE achieves stronger cross-domain generalization than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [55] [SR-JEPA: Learning Predictive Latent State in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05774)
*Zihan Zhou,Qifu Wen,Xi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn by predicting latent representations of missing observations, yet many masked JEPAs are evaluated primarily through the encoders they produce. We ask what a trained predictive pathway itself infers when an entire entity is absent from a native 3D scene. We introduce SR-JEPA, a point-native JEPA for scene-scale point clouds whose original frozen predictive pathway can be queried at a supplied location. At evaluation, every point of one object is removed before encoding and replaced by the same shape-free 32-point query at its centroid. Training uses only self-contained 3D EMA targets: no reconstruction, semantic labels, language, or lifted 2D features. On 5,953 held-out ARKitScenes objects, the imputed latent reaches 43.13% semantic-identity macro accuracy, 22.18 points above the strongest floor. Randomizing the prediction path removes 9.78 points, while substituting matched donor context removes 21.98 points. On 8,570 Sr3D support pairs, the full latent reaches 41.15 AP; identity decoded from the missing-object latent, combined with anchor identity and geometry, reaches 39.37 AP, leaving an unresolved 1.78-point residual. These results reveal a queryable, compositional 3D predictive state: the model completes context-dependent entity content, which downstream computation combines with metric geometry.

</details>


### [56] [Vorch-Director: Interactive World Story Model via Noise-Aware Error Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05776)
*Lisai Zhang,Yidi Wu,Qi Liu,Xin Ma,Yang Ding,Gang Yue,Siqian Yang,Jingyuan Chen,Lin Ma,Yaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vorch-Director是一种噪声级别感知的残差校正策略，用于解决自回归音频-视频生成中的误差累积问题，通过在训练时注入与去噪过程噪声级别匹配的合成误差，显著提高了长视频生成的稳定性和音视频同步性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归音视频生成方法在训练时依赖于干净的真实历史数据，而推理时依赖模型自身生成的历史数据。这种不匹配会导致累积误差，引发身份漂移、过度平滑和音视频不同步等问题。虽然近期方法通过重用预测残差作为合成腐蚀来减少这种不匹配，但残差校正的效果高度依赖于产生残差时的流匹配噪声级别，而现有方法忽略了这一点。

Method: Vorch-Director的核心是噪声级别感知的残差校正策略。它将每个残差与其产生的噪声级别相关联，并在训练过程中从匹配的噪声状态注入残差，使注入的误差与去噪过程对齐。该方法构建于音频-视觉LTX-2扩散变换器之上，并引入任务嵌入来区分历史视频、参考图像和目标视频，从而实现统一的条件生成。此外，它结合了干净条件锚定和混合任务训练，支持多镜头、多主体、参考引导的音频-视觉长视频生成。

Result: 在ST-Bench基准和论文新提出的长时域音视频基准（包含质量漂移和长程一致性指标）上进行的广泛实验表明，Vorch-Director与强基线方法相比，显著提高了生成的稳定性和音视频保真度。

Conclusion: Vorch-Director通过创新的噪声级别感知残差校正策略，有效解决了自回归音视频生成中的误差累积和训练-推理不匹配问题。结合任务嵌入等设计，该方法支持高质量、长时域的多条件音视频生成，为该领域提供了新的解决方案和评估基准。

Abstract: Autoregressive continuation provides a natural path toward minute-scale audio-visual generation by repeatedly extending a short-window generator conditioned on previously generated video and audio. However, models are trained on clean ground-truth histories, while inference relies on their own generated histories, where accumulated errors cause identity drift, over-smoothing, and audio-visual desynchronization. Recent methods reduce this mismatch by reusing prediction residuals as synthetic corruption, but we observe that the effectiveness of residual correction critically depends on the flow-matching noise level at which residuals are produced. We propose Vorch-Director, a noise-level-aware residual correction strategy that associates each residual with its originating noise level and injects residuals from matched noise regimes during training. By aligning injected errors with the denoising process, Vorch-Director produces more realistic autoregressive histories while retaining efficient teacher-forcing training. Built on the audio-visual LTX-2 diffusion transformer, Vorch-Director further introduces task embeddings to distinguish historical video, reference images, and target video, enabling unified conditioning for long-horizon generation. Together with a clean conditioning sink and mixed-task training, Vorch-Director supports multi-shot, multi-subject, reference-guided audio-visual long-video generation. We evaluate Vorch-Director on ST-Bench and introduce a new long-horizon audio-visual benchmark with metrics for quality drift and long-range consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved stability and audio-visual fidelity over strong baselines.

</details>


### [57] [Evidence-Driven Dynamic Visual Selector for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05780)
*Bo Zhang,Wenxin Wang,Feng Chen,Zhihao Zhang,Zixuan Wang,Changsheng Li,Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviSelect是一种基于目标多模态大语言模型（MLLM）内部注意力证据的细粒度动态视觉选择框架，用于提升长视频理解的效率与性能。它通过稀疏预填充和分布感知的动态采样，显著减少计算开销并保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法主要依赖外部代理评分器和刚性启发式规则进行帧选择，这导致与目标MLLM的内在证据不对齐，并且无法适应视频中非均匀的时空信息密度，从而限制了推理效率并增加了计算成本。

Method: 1. 证据探测：通过稀疏预填充（使用高度压缩的视觉输入和稀疏注意力）高效近似目标MLLM的注意力图，作为结构化先验。2. 动态选择器设计：基于该先验导出三个互补注意力组件，设计轻量级选择器，可精确定位查询相关时间戳，并自适应调整局部采样率与空间分辨率。3. 策略优化：将选择器建模为随机策略，采用GRPO算法，在联合准确性-效率奖励下进行优化，鼓励模型根据信息密度动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在三个长视频理解基准测试中，EviSelect相较于现有方法展现出更优性能，同时将所选视觉token减少约50%，并实现3.9倍的端到端推理速度提升。

Conclusion: EviSelect通过直接利用目标MLLM的内部注意力证据指导动态视觉采样，有效解决了现有方法存在的证据不对齐与采样僵化问题，在显著降低计算开销的同时提升了长视频理解任务的性能，为高效视频理解提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advancements in MLLM-based long-form video understanding have mitigated inference-time computational cost and limited context lengths by selecting query-relevant frames. However, existing approaches predominantly rely on external proxy scorers and rigid heuristic rules, inevitably suffering from misalignment with the target MLLM's intrinsic evidence and failing to accommodate the non-uniform spatiotemporal information density. In this paper, we propose a fine-grained dynamic visual selection framework named EviSelect, grounded in the target MLLM internal attention evidence. Our method efficiently probes visual evidence via sparse prefilling as a structured prior to guide distribution-aware dynamic sampling. Specifically, we efficiently approximate attention maps of the target MLLM using highly compressed visual inputs and sparse attention, well-aligned to the full counterpart. Conditioned on three complementary attention components derived from this prior, we design a lightweight selector that not only precisely locates query-relevant timestamps but also adaptively adjusts the local sampling rate and spatial resolution. To enable evidence-conditioned spatiotemporal sampling, we formulate the selector as a stochastic policy and optimize it via GRPO under a joint accuracy--efficiency reward. By rewarding correct predictions under lower visual cost through group-relative comparisons, our method encourages the policy to allocate computation dynamically according to the information density of each video. Across three long video understanding benchmarks, EviSelect achieves superior performance compared to existing methods while reducing selected visual tokens by about 50\% and achieving a 3.9x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [58] [VSMP-IMU: Video-Grounded Semantic Motion Programs for Sensor-Aware Synthetic IMU Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05782)
*Lala Shakti Swarup Ray,Vitor Fortes Rey,Mengxi Liu,Paul Lukowicz,Bo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VSMP-IMU框架，通过结合视频驱动与文本驱动的优势，利用结构化语义运动程序生成可控且与可穿戴设备相关的合成IMU数据，显著提升了低资源、类别不平衡和跨被试场景下的人体活动识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备的人体活动识别常受限于标注传感器数据的稀缺性，尤其在资源不足、类别不平衡和跨被试泛化场景下。现有合成IMU数据生成方法面临权衡：视频驱动方法视觉基础好但易受姿态估计误差影响，文本驱动方法可控但弱于实际执行方式。因此需要一种能同时解决这些问题的合成数据生成框架。

Method: 提出VSMP-IMU框架，基于结构化语义运动程序（SMP）实现可控的合成IMU生成。该框架从输入视频中提取并增强SMP，使用SMP合成运动，再将运动转换为虚拟IMU信号，并将其与目标可穿戴设备域对齐。SMP将活动定义语义与标签保持变异分离，实现结构化控制。

Result: 在五个公开IMU-HAR数据集上进行留一被试评估。VSMP-IMU平均Macro-F1达到78.33%，相比仅真实数据训练提升9.77%，相比最强合成基线提升4.04%。在低资源设置下，相比仅真实数据训练提升18.54%，相比最强合成基线平均提升超过6%。在不平衡数据集的长尾评估中，尾部类别Macro-F1相比仅真实数据训练提升19.86%，相比现有最优方法提升4.76%。

Conclusion: 结构化视频语义为可控、与可穿戴设备相关的合成传感器数据生成提供了实用基础。VSMP-IMU通过结合视觉基础和语义控制，在多种挑战性设置下有效提升了HAR性能，为解决标注数据稀缺问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Wearable human activity recognition (HAR) is often limited by the scarcity of labeled sensor data, especially in low-resource, class-imbalanced, and subject-generalization settings. Synthetic IMU generation can reduce this dependency and enhance HAR machine learning model's performance, but existing approaches face a trade-off without addressing all factors: video-driven methods are visually grounded but sensitive to pose-estimation errors, while text-driven methods are controllable but often weakly grounded in how activities are actually performed. We present VSMP-IMU, a video-grounded framework for controllable synthetic IMU generation based on a structured Semantic Motion Program (SMP), which separates activity-defining semantics from label-preserving variation. Given an input video, VSMP-IMU extracts and augments an SMP, uses it to synthesize motion, converts the motion into virtual IMU signals, and grounds the resulting signals to the target wearable domain. We evaluate VSMP-IMU against state-of-the-art synthetic data generation methods on five public IMU-HAR datasets under leave-one-person-out evaluation. VSMP-IMU achieves an average Macro-F1 of 78.33%, improving over real-only training by 9.77% and over the strongest prior synthetic baseline by 4.04%. In low-resource settings with reduced training data-samples, it improves over real-only training by 18.54% and over the strongest prior synthetic baselines by more than 6% on average. Under long-tail evaluation in imbalanced datasets, it improves tail-class Macro-F1 by 19.86% over Real-only training and by 4.76% over SOTA. These results show that structured video-grounded semantics provide a practical foundation for controllable, wearable-relevant synthetic sensor data generation.

</details>


### [59] [KVAE: Family of Tokenizers for Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05798)
*Andrey Shutkin,Denis Parkhomenko,Ivan Kirillov,Kirill Chernyshev,Kirill Malakhov,Ilia Vasiliev,Ilia Trushkin,Valeriya Kobenko,David Chikovani,Alexander Ivanov,Azat Saginbaev,Egor Silvestrov,Ivan Mikheev,Konstantin Zakharov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本报告介绍了KVAE系列tokenizer（包括音频、图像和视频模型），它们专为文本条件生成设计，在重建和生成质量上达到或超越了当前顶尖开源tokenizer的水平，并公开了代码与训练细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜扩散模型（LDM）依赖tokenizer将输入信号映射为压缩表示，tokenizer的性能直接影响学习速度、合成样本的质量，并为后续应用奠定基础，因此设计高效的tokenizer至关重要。

Method: 设计并实现了三种针对不同模态的KVAE tokenizer：KVAE-Audio（连续全频段48kHz音频tokenizer，50Hz潜空间64通道）、KVAE-3D（两种因果视频tokenizer，压缩比分别为4x16x16和4x8x8）、KVAE-2D（图像tokenizer，压缩比为8倍，32通道）。通过系统训练、模型选择和消融实验进行了验证。

Result: 在重建指标（PSNR、LPIPS、PESQ等）和生成指标（Fréchet距离、CLIP分数、CLAP分数等）以及主观评估上，KVAE系列tokenizer的性能匹配或超越了现有的前沿开源tokenizer（如来自Wan-2.2、HunyuanVideo-1.5、FLUX.2、MovieGen、StableAudio和MMAudio的VAE）。

Conclusion: KVAE系列tokenizer是有效的音频、图像和视频压缩与生成工具，其性能具有竞争力。研究通过公开代码、训练细节和设计选择，降低了开发难度，有助于推动相关领域的研究与应用发展。

Abstract: Latent diffusion modeling (LDM), a prominent paradigm, utilizes tokenizers to map input signal to compressed representation. This dependency positions tokenizer as an integral part of generation process itself, since it affects learning speed, quality of synthesized samples and lay foundation for later applications. This report presents series of KVAE tokenizers for audio, image and video, all designed for subsequent text-conditioned generation: KVAE-Audio, a continuous full-band 48 kHz tokenizer with a 50 Hz latent of 64 channels; KVAE-3D -- two causal video tokenizers for 4x16x16 and 4x8x8 compression; KVAE-2D, an image model, compressing input by factor of 8 with 32 channels. We demonstrate that reconstruction (PSNR, LPIPS, PESQ, etc.) and generation results on objective (Frechet Distance, CLIP score, CLAP score, etc.) and subjective (side-by-side evaluation) metrics matches or surpasses frontier opensource tokenizers, such as VAEs from Wan-2.2, HunyuanVideo-1.5, FLUX.2, MovieGen, StableAudio and MMAudio. Considering difficulty of development, we share with community training details, model selection method and ablation on design choices. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae and https://github.com/kandinskylab/kvae-audio.

</details>


### [60] [Vorch-Omni: Multi-Task Orchestration of Sight and Sound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05803)
*Vorch Team,Xiaoyu Chen,Yang Ding,Cong Han,Menglin Han,Yuxin Hong,Jiebo Hou,Zequn Jie,Xiang Li,Jing Liu,Qi Liu,Yulei Lu,Siyuan Luo,Lin Ma,Xin Ma,Yinlong Qian,Peng Shi,Fang Wan,Siqi Wang,Yaohui Wang,Yaole Wang,Yidi Wu,Siqian Yang,Mingyu Yin,Haoran Yu,Gang Yue,Lisai Zhang,Yuting Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vorch-Omni是一个统一的多任务音视频生成框架，采用“任意条件到任意输出”的公式，支持文本到视频、文本到音视频、图像条件生成、时间扩展、音频驱动生成等超过10个任务，通过token级条件掩码和任务标识符灵活处理不同信号类型，为通用音视频生成提供可扩展基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成视频模型方法依赖于碎片化的特定任务模型，缺乏通用性。音视频联合生成进一步增加了挑战，因为不同模态的条件和输出配置多样。需要一个通用模型来区分异构目标、源和参考信号，同时减少任务间的干扰。

Method: Vorch-Omni基于“任意条件到任意输出”的公式，灵活处理视频和音频信号。使用token级条件掩码和任务标识符区分目标、源内容和参考，位置类型分离时间上下文和独立条件。采用互补视觉条件路径：视觉语言模型解释采样帧和文本指令，视频VAE将条件编码为潜在token。构建分布式数据管道整理多样化的时对齐音视频片段。基于单流匹配扩散transformer构建，无特定任务架构更改。

Result: 该框架支持超过10个任务，包括文本到视频、文本到音视频、图像和参考条件生成、时间扩展、音频驱动生成、视频转换和音视频编辑。提供了一个可扩展的通用音视频生成和操作基础。

Conclusion: Vorch-Omni是一个统一的多任务框架，通过灵活的信号处理和互补的条件路径，实现了多种音视频合成任务，为通用音视频生成奠定了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video modeling have enabled diverse generation, reference-based synthesis, extension, and editing, but existing approaches often rely on fragmented task-specific models. A general model must distinguish heterogeneous target, source, and reference signals to determine what to generate, preserve, or use as guidance, while reducing interference among tasks. Joint audio-visual generation further increases this challenge by introducing diverse conditioning and output configurations across modalities. We present Vorch-Omni, a unified multi-task framework for audio-visual synthesis based on an arbitrary-condition-to-arbitrary-output formulation. It flexibly treats video and audio signals as either conditioning inputs or generation targets. Token-level conditioning masks and task identifiers distinguish targets, source content, and references, while position types separate temporal context from independent conditions. To capture semantic and structural information, Vorch-Omni employs complementary visual conditioning pathways: a vision-language model interprets sampled frames with text instructions, and a video VAE encodes conditions into latent tokens for direct guidance. We further build a distributed data pipeline to curate diverse temporally aligned audio-visual clips, generate structured captions and metadata, and balance heterogeneous task distributions. Built on a single flow-matching diffusion transformer without task-specific architectural changes, Vorch-Omni supports over 10 tasks, including text-to-video, text-to-audio-video, image- and reference-conditioned generation, temporal extension, audio-driven generation, video transformation, and audio-visual editing. This unified framework provides a scalable foundation for general-purpose audio-visual generation and manipulation.

</details>


### [61] [Ordered Diffusion for 3D Human Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05804)
*Mattia Masiero,Ilya A. Petrov,Daniel Cremers,Gerard Pons-Moll,Riccardo Marin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ODin方法，将3D人体配准从回归任务重新定义为生成式任务，通过扩散过程建模配准中的不确定性，从而生成更合理、更稳健的对齐结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D人体配准方法将其视为回归任务，假设存在唯一正确的对齐。但实际上，由于采集噪声、遮挡和软组织动态变化等因素，人体扫描数据存在固有的模糊性。回归方法通常只能收敛到一个平均预测，往往无法代表合理的几何形状，因此需要一种能够建模这种不确定性的新方法。

Method: ODin将配准建模为一个3D扩散过程，该过程在保持模板点云语义（通过一致的点顺序）的同时，生成与目标几何对齐的点云。该方法利用全局、局部和位置条件引导每个点移动到正确位置。

Result: 实验表明，ODin这种生成式方法不仅优于其回归基线方法，而且达到了新的state of the art水平，在超越现有高度工程化方法的同时，将配准时间减少了三分之二。

Conclusion: ODin通过将配准建模为生成式分布而非单一回归目标，成功解决了人体扫描数据中的固有模糊性问题，在精度和效率上都取得了显著提升，为3D人体配准任务提供了更稳健和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: 3D human registration has historically been treated as a regression task, assuming a unique ground-truth alignment exists between the template and an input point cloud. In reality, acquisition noise, occlusions, and unknown soft tissue dynamics introduce inherent ambiguity into human scans. Regression-based methods consequently converge to an average prediction, often failing to represent a plausible geometry. In our work, we embrace such uncertainty by modeling the registration as a distribution of alignments. We propose ODin, which formulates registration as a 3D diffusion process that generates a point cloud aligned with the target geometry while preserving template semantics through consistent point ordering. To achieve this, ODin relies on global, local, and positional conditioning, guiding each point to its correct location. Our experiments demonstrate that such a generative formulation not only outperforms its regression-based baseline, but also establishes a new state of the art, surpassing highly engineered methods while reducing the registration time by two-thirds. Pre-trained models and code are available at https://riccardomarin.github.io/odin/.

</details>


### [62] [STAIL: Semantic Text-Anchored Incremental Learning for Medical Imaging via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05808)
*Songpan Gao,Yajie Zhang,Guanxing Chen,Jiayu Qian,Zhenzhen Liu,Shijun Li,Xiaowei Zhu,Yao Hu,Kay Chen Tan,Yu-An Huang,Shiqi Wang,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像分析中深度学习模型的灾难性遗忘问题，本研究提出了一种名为语义文本锚定增量学习（STAIL）的新框架，通过轻量级的非对称语义巩固缓冲区和基于大型语言模型的语义锚定机制，在最小化存储开销的同时显著提升持续学习性能并减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态临床环境中，深度学习模型在适应新任务时会遭遇严重的灾难性遗忘。现有增量学习方法依赖于重放原始图像，这不仅存储开销大、存在隐私风险，而且难以用稀疏样本准确表征原始数据分布。受人类认知机制启发，需要一种更高效、隐私友好且能有效捕捉数据语义本质的方法来应对这一挑战。

Method: 本文提出了语义文本锚定增量学习（STAIL）框架。其核心创新包括：1. 非对称语义巩固缓冲区（SCB）：存储少量图像锚点和丰富的文本描述，以极低的存储成本实现对旧任务数据分布的密集语义重建。2. LLM派生的语义锚定机制（LSAM）：利用冻结的大型语言模型所具有的稳定语义空间作为先验知识，显式地将模型不断演变的视觉特征锚定到稳定的文本表示上，从而在宏观和微观层面约束模型的学习过程，平衡可塑性与稳定性。

Result: 在眼底、超声和X射线三个异质性医学影像数据集上的广泛实验表明，STAIL作为一个即插即用的模块，能够全面增强多种现有增量学习基线的性能。具体而言，它平均提升了2.24%的AAA-AUC（衡量持续性能）和3.55%的BWT-AUC（衡量遗忘程度）。

Conclusion: STAIL框架通过引入轻量级语义缓冲区和基于大语言模型的语义锚定机制，为医学图像分析中的持续学习任务提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。该方法在显著降低存储与隐私风险的同时，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，提升了模型在序列化临床任务中的长期性能，具有很高的实用价值和应用潜力。

Abstract: Deep learning models applied to medical image analysis suffer from severe catastrophic forgetting when continually adapting to new clinical tasks in dynamic environments. Mainstream incremental learning methods typically mitigate this by rehearsing raw historical images. However, this pixel-level rehearsal incurs significant storage overhead, raises privacy concerns, and fails to adequately capture the true data distribution with sparse exemplars. Inspired by human cognitive mechanisms, we propose a novel framework termed Semantic Text-Anchored Incremental Learning (STAIL) for sequential clinical tasks. To overcome the rehearsal bottleneck, STAIL introduces an asymmetric semantic consolidation buffer (SCB). By incorporating a minimal set of image anchors and extensive textual descriptions, the SCB enables dense semantic reconstruction of old tasks at a minimal storage cost. Furthermore, we design an LLM-derived Semantic Anchoring Mechanism (LSAM) that leverages the stable semantic space of frozen large language models as developmental priors. This mechanism explicitly anchors evolving visual features to textual representations, guiding and constraining plasticity and stability at both macroscopic and microscopic levels. Extensive experiments across three heterogeneous medical datasets, covering fundus, ultrasound, and X-ray imaging, demonstrate that STAIL acts as a highly effective plug-and-play module. It comprehensively enhances the performance of various existing baselines, achieving average gains of 2.24\% in AAA-AUC for sustained performance and 3.55\% in BWT-AUC for reduced forgetting. Code is available.

</details>


### [63] [Energy-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05811)
*Haoyang Tong,Yu He,Fang Li,Lichen Ma,Jingling Fu,Dong Chen,Zhen Chen,Junshi Huang,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出能量引导流匹配（EG-FM），通过动态端点实现从粗到细的生成轨迹，显著提升了像素空间图像生成的质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间生成模型需要在高维空间中联合学习全局结构与精细细节，而标准流匹配依赖固定端点，频谱演化需隐式学习。本文旨在显式建模从低频到高频的生成过程，以优化生成轨迹。

Method: EG-FM用热核过滤的动态端点替代固定端点，该端点从低频图像平滑演化至清晰图像。高频成分的释放由图像特定的能量引导调度控制，从而调整流匹配的速度。此方法无需修改骨干网络或训练数据，计算开销极小。

Result: 在ImageNet类条件图像生成（256×256）中，EG-FM在200和600训练轮次下分别达到1.55和1.45的FID；在512×512分辨率下经40轮次微调后FID为1.58。在文本到图像生成中，取得0.85的GenEval分数和83.9的DPG-Bench分数。

Conclusion: EG-FM通过能量引导的动态端点显式建模粗到细生成轨迹，在低计算开销下显著提升图像生成质量，适用于类条件生成和文本到图像生成任务。

Abstract: Pixel-space generative models bypass lossy latent compression, yet necessitate joint learning of global structure and fine-grained details in a high-dimensional space. Standard flow matching interpolates noise toward a fixed clean-image endpoint, leaving the spectral evolution to be learned implicitly. In this paper, we introduce Energy-Guided Flow Matching(EG-FM) that explicitly models a coarse-to-fine generative trajectory by moving endpoint. Specifically, EG-FM replaces the fixed endpoint with a heat-kernel-filtered endpoint that evolves smoothly from low-frequency image to clean image.The fraction of high-frequency signal in moving endpoint is released by an image-specific energy-guided scheduling, leading to the re-targeting of velocity in flow matching.Our framework requires no adaptation of the backbone and training data, bringing negligible cost on the training and inference stages. In our experiment, EG-FM consistently achieves lower FID on the ImageNet class-conditional image generation task at $256 \times 256$ with fewer epochs, reaching an FID of 1.55 at 200 epochs and 1.45 at 600 epochs. We continue training the generation task on the setting of $512 \times 512$ resolution, yielding a FID of 1.58 after only 40 high-resolution adaptation epochs.Furthermore, we transfer EG-FM on text-to-image generation and achieve 0.85 on GenEval score and 83.9 on DPG-Bench. Code is available at https://github.com/ysng123/EG-FM.

</details>


### [64] [Bayesian adaptively-weighted ensembles for few-shot abdominal segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05815)
*Abbas Al-Sabbagh,Shalom F. Mushtaq,Tomás M. da Silva,Kushagra Soni,Binawei Gbamila,Sri Atluri,Qianye Yang,Yipeng Hu,Claire C. Villette,Shaheer U. Saeed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对少样本学习在医学图像分割中的应用，提出一种贝叶斯自适应加权集成框架，以解决不同算法在不同解剖目标和机构间性能差异大的问题。该框架能根据目标域动态调整模型贡献权重，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本学习算法在医学图像分割中表现出互补性，性能随解剖目标和数据来源机构不同而变化。现有集成方法采用固定权重，无法根据目标域调整，限制了在标注数据稀缺和存在域偏移场景下的部署。

Method: 提出一个贝叶斯自适应加权集成框架：首先使用小规模标注支持集适应多个少样本分割算法；然后通过贝叶斯优化在目标域验证集上自动确定最优集成权重；最后将权重固定，应用于来自目标域的未见查询图像的预测融合。

Result: 在跨机构男性骨盆结构数据集上，使用留出的解剖结构和机构模拟标注稀缺与域偏移。结果表明，该框架在统计上显著优于单个少样本学习器、固定权重集成、从头训练基线及近期先进的集成方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过动态调整模型贡献以适应目标解剖结构和机构域，为在严重标注约束下将分割系统部署到新临床站点提供了一种实用机制。

Abstract: Few-shot learning has emerged as a promising approach for anatomical segmentation when labelled data are scarce. However, different few-shot learning algorithms exhibit complementary strengths and weaknesses, with performance varying across anatomical targets and institutions. Existing few-shot segmentation ensembles, that combine predictions from multiple algorithms, typically employ fixed weighting schemes and therefore cannot adjust model contributions according to the target domain. In this work, we propose a Bayesian adaptively-weighted ensemble framework for segmentation under label scarcity and domain shift. Multiple few-shot segmentation algorithms are first adapted using a small labelled support set. Bayesian optimisation is then used to automatically identify ensemble weights that maximise segmentation performance on a target-domain validation set. The learned weights are subsequently fixed and applied to combine predictions on previously unseen query images from the target domain. The proposed framework is evaluated on the Cross-institution Male Pelvic Structures dataset using held-out anatomical structures and institutions to simulate simultaneous label scarcity and institutional domain shift. Results demonstrate statistically significant improvements over individual few-shot learners, fixed-weight ensembles, training-from-scratch baselines and recent state-of-the-art ensembling approaches. By adapting model contributions to the target anatomy and institutional domain, the proposed framework provides a practical mechanism for deploying segmentation systems to new clinical sites under severe annotation constraints.

</details>


### [65] [Controllable Clothing: Precise Labels and Generation for Virtual Try-On with Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05834)
*Max Rehman Linder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于标签引导的虚拟试衣图像生成新方法，通过开源AI模型增强数据并训练适配器，以生成多样化、可控且真实的服装图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决虚拟试衣中图像生成的真实性问题，确保生成的图像准确反映服装的真实版型和样式，避免误导消费者，尤其对零售商等终端用户至关重要。

Method: 利用新的开源AI模型为图像数据添加结构化标签（如长度、风格），并将这些标签与服装图像配对，训练适配器（adapter）来引导图像生成模型。

Result: 模型能够生成更多样化的服装图像，并且用户可以通过标签控制生成图像的属性，从而获得更符合预期且真实的虚拟试衣效果。

Conclusion: 该方法通过引入可控的标签引导机制，有效提升了虚拟试衣图像生成的多样性和真实性，为零售等应用场景提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In this technical report, I present a new method for guiding image generation in the context of Virtual- Try-On (VITON). The proposed method leverages new open source Ai models to augment the image data with labels, such as lengths and styles. By training adapters with these labels paired with images of the garments, the model can produce a more diverse set of images that the user can control. For the end user, such as a retailer, this means that they can assure that the produced image is as true to the true fit as possible, not misleading consumers

</details>


### [66] [Accurate Localization of Road Traffic Objects on the Road Plane Using Surveillance Camera Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05840)
*Jan Gawroński,Witold Czajewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何感知的两阶段方法，利用单目路边监控摄像头实现更准确的车辆定位，显著优于传统的边界框中心定位法，尤其适用于透视畸变和视差较大的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的车辆定位方法通常从检测器边界框的中心估计车辆位置，但由于透视畸变和视差（尤其在摄像头位置较高或车辆较大时），会产生较大误差，影响智能交通系统、交通监控和冲突分析的准确性。

Method: 提出两阶段几何感知定位流程：1) 使用YOLO26检测器进行车辆检测；2) 使用一个专用的ResNet34回归网络，预测车辆底部在路平面上的投影所对应的四个角点。最终位置通过计算预测四边形的几何中心获得。该方法首先在CARLA生成的合成数据上训练，然后在DAIR-V2X的真实路边图像数据集上进行微调。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上的实验表明，该方法相比朴素的边界框中心定位法有显著改进。在DAIR-V2X数据集上，图像空间定位平均误差从31.77像素降至15.30像素，相对改进51.8%，中位数误差降至4.29像素。在地面平面误差方面，对于中等距离车辆，中位数误差从5.52米降至0.90米；对于远距离车辆，从8.67米降至1.84米。结果还表明，检测器边界框周围的上下文信息对几何定位至关重要，方法在远距离车辆和受强透视畸变及视差影响的几何复杂案例上获得了最大收益。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用车辆检测边界框周围的上下文信息进行几何感知建模，能有效提升单目摄像头车辆定位的准确性，尤其在传统方法因透视畸变和视差而误差较大的场景下（如远距离、高摄像头）表现突出，为智能交通系统提供了更可靠的定位基础。

Abstract: Accurate vehicle localization from monocular roadside surveillance cameras is important for intelligent transportation systems, traffic monitoring, and traffic conflict analysis. Standard approaches often estimate vehicle position from the center of the detector bounding box, which can produce large errors due to perspective distortion and parallax, especially for elevated cameras and large vehicles.
  This paper proposes a two-stage geometry-aware localization pipeline that estimates the projection of the vehicle footprint onto the road plane. First, vehicles are detected using a YOLO26-based detector. Second, a dedicated ResNet34 regression network predicts four corner points corresponding to the projected vehicle base. The final position is computed as the geometric center of the predicted quadrilateral.
  The method was trained on synthetic data generated in CARLA and fine-tuned on real-world roadside imagery from DAIR-V2X. Experiments on synthetic and real data showed clear improvements over naive bounding-box-center localization. On DAIR-V2X, the mean image-space localization error decreased from 31.77 px to 15.30 px, a 51.8% improvement, while the median error decreased to 4.29 px. Median ground-plane error for medium-range vehicles decreased from 5.52 m to 0.90 m, and for far-range vehicles from 8.67 m to 1.84 m.
  The results also show that contextual information surrounding the detector bounding box is important for geometric localization. The largest gains were observed for distant vehicles and geometrically challenging cases affected by strong perspective distortion and parallax.

</details>


### [67] [Overcoming Attention Drift: Homogeneity-Heterogeneity Guided Feature Aggregation for Low-Light Remote Sensing Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05843)
*Yaozi Zhong,Xingxing Yang,Shaohui Mei,Mingyang Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Restoring high-fidelity remote sensing imagery from extreme low-light degradation is indispensable for reliable Earth observation and downstream machine vision. However, under severe noise and illumination corruption, existing methods suffer from attention drift, erroneously aggregating features across distinct physical boundaries and causing severe structural blurring and color distortion. To address this, we propose HALO, a dual-prior-driven enhancement framework that formulates enhancement as a guided feature aggregation problem driven by foundation model priors. Specifically, an illumination-invariant semantic prior provides regional homogeneity as a positive bias for content-consistent aggregation, while a pseudo-3D topological prior provides boundary heterogeneity as a negative penalty to strictly prevent cross-boundary confusion. To cooperatively incorporate these two priors, we propose a Homogeneity-Heterogeneity Cooperative Attention Module (H2CAM) to resolve feature conflicts during cross-modal prior fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HALO achieves state-of-the-art performance across 8 challenging synthetic and real-world remote sensing benchmarks, significantly improving physical boundary sharpness and color fidelity while maximizing the preservation of discriminative features for downstream Earth observation tasks.

</details>


### [68] [Curia-MAE: Multi-Modal Multi-Anatomy MAE Pre-Training for 3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05844)
*Théo Danielou,Antoine Saporta,Léo Alberge,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Curia-MAE，一个用于放射学影像的多模态、多解剖部位卷积掩码自编码器基础模型，通过改进预训练目标，在冻结编码器设置下显著提升了密集预测（特别是3D分割）任务的性能，并在标记数据稀缺的病灶分割任务上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的放射学基础模型在密集预测任务（如3D分割）上评估不足，且当编码器保持冻结时，其性能仍落后于从头训练的先进模型（如nnU-Net）。这限制了基础模型在临床工作流中的实际应用潜力，因此需要一种能提升冻结编码器性能、并有效利用稀缺标注数据的预训练方法。

Method: 在标准的卷积掩码自编码器预训练基础上，引入了三项改进：1. 鲁棒重建目标，以增强模型对噪声和扰动的抵抗力；2. 特征正则化器，以优化特征表示；3. 局部-全局相似性目标，以捕获多尺度信息。在此基础上，构建了Curia-MAE模型，该模型在一个包含30万张CT和MRI图像的大型多模态、多解剖部位数据集上进行预训练。

Result: 在八个涵盖不同解剖部位和病灶的分割基准测试中，Curia-MAE在编码器冻结的设置下，性能超越了强大的MAE基线模型。在需要全参数微调的场景下，其性能具有竞争力。尤其在标注数据稀缺的病灶分割任务上，Curia-MAE展现出明显的优势。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过改进的预训练目标（Curia-MAE），可以在单一冻结编码器的基础上，有效支持多样化的分割任务，这显著降低了模型适应和部署于临床工作流的成本。该模型权重将公开，有望推动放射学AI应用的发展。

Abstract: Radiology foundation models learn transferable representations that can be adapted to new tasks by training only small layers on top of a frozen encoder. Dense prediction tasks such as 3D segmentation are, however, underrepresented in their evaluation, and, with the encoder kept frozen, pre-trained models still fall short of nnU-Net, the state-of-the-art reference trained from scratch. To close this gap we extend convolutional MAE pre-training with a robust reconstruction objective, a feature regularizer, and a local-global similarity objective. Using this method, we propose Curia-MAE, a multi-modal, multi-anatomy MAE model pre-trained on 300,000 CT and MRI images covering a large number of anatomical sites. On eight anatomy- and lesion-focused segmentation benchmarks, Curia-MAE improves frozen-encoder performance over a strong MAE baseline, while remaining competitive under full finetuning and superior on lesion tasks, where labeled data is scarce. These results indicate that a single frozen encoder can be reused across diverse segmentation tasks, reducing the cost of adapting and deploying such models in clinical workflows. We will make our pre-trained model weights publicly available.

</details>


### [69] [DTRNet: Dual Text-Radical Decoding for Handwritten Chinese Text Recognition with Faked Character Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05848)
*Runrui Li,Lin Zhu,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对K-12教育场景中手写中文文本识别不仅需转录还需检测伪造字符的问题，现有方法存在局限性，本文提出了双文本-偏旁解码框架DTRNet，实现了行级伪造字符检测，兼顾效率、识别性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型局限于预定义正常字符集，无法显式识别伪造字符。字符级伪造字符检测方法能提供可解释的结构证据但效率低下；行级方法效率较高，但过度依赖置信度分数，导致易漏检且缺乏显式结构证据。核心挑战在于如何在保持行级效率的同时，保留独立于上下文推理的字符结构证据。

Method: 提出DTRNet，一个用于行级伪造字符检测的双文本-偏旁解码框架。该框架将上下文感知的文本识别与字符级结构验证解耦，包括两个分支：1）文本分支：执行行级文本转录；2）偏旁分支：预测合法的表意文字描述序列（IDS），用于基于词典的伪造字符判断。在推理阶段，进一步引入了IDS引导的置信度调整（IGCA）方法，利用结构证据来优化文本预测结果。

Result: 实验证明，DTRNet能够有效检测伪造字符，同时保持强大的文本识别性能，并提供可解释的偏旁级结构证据。

Conclusion: DTRNet框架通过双分支解码设计，成功解决了现有字符级与行级方法在效率和结构证据方面的互补性局限，实现了行级效率与可解释的字符结构证据的结合，为教育场景中的手写中文伪造字符检测提供了有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: In K-12 educational scenarios, handwritten Chinese text recognition should not only transcribe student writing, but also detect faked characters. However, existing recognition models are usually confined to a predefined set of normal characters and therefore cannot explicitly identify faked characters. Existing detection methods exhibit complementary limitations: character-level methods provide interpretable structural evidence but suffer from low efficiency, whereas line-level methods are efficient but rely heavily on confidence scores, making them prone to missed detections and lacking explicit structural evidence. Thus, the key challenge is to preserve character-structural evidence independent of contextual inference while maintaining line-level efficiency. To this end, we propose DTRNet, a dual Text-Radical decoding framework for line-level faked character detection. DTRNet decouples context-aware text recognition from character-wise structural verification, where the text branch performs line-level transcription and the radical branch predicts legal Ideographic Description Sequences (IDS) for lexicon-based faked character judgment. We further introduce IDS-Guided Confidence Adjustment (IGCA) to refine text predictions using structural evidence during inference. Experimental results demonstrate that DTRNet effectively detects faked characters while maintaining strong recognition performance and providing interpretable radical-level evidence. Code, checkpoints, and the processed dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/BNU-ERC-ITEA/DTRNet.

</details>


### [70] [D-CLOT: Double Closed Loop Optimal Transport for Unsupervised Action Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05877)
*Elena Bueno-Benito,Mariella Dimiccoli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了D-CLOT和D-CLOT_B两种方法，通过引入图约束模块和动作原型重估计来解决现有基于最优传输（OT）的无监督动作分割方法中存在的“表征-原型不一致”瓶颈。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试上显著提升了分割性能，并首次为更细粒度的Assembly101基准建立了无监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输（OT）的无监督动作分割方法存在一个核心瓶颈：定义OT成本的潜在动作原型仅通过伪标签损失的梯度演化，而未从精炼后的帧表征几何中重新估计。这种“表征-原型不一致”导致在动作转换模糊区域以及针对短时或不频繁动作时性能下降。本文旨在通过动态更新动作原型，使其与精炼后的帧表征保持一致，从而解决此问题。

Method: 本文在CLOT框架基础上进行了两项核心改进：1. 引入一个图约束模块，该模块通过保持编码器输出的局部邻域几何结构，来正则化OT精炼后的帧和片段表征。2. 设计一个动作嵌入精炼步骤，该步骤会周期性地将动作原型重新锚定到稳定的表征几何上。基于此，作者提出了两种具体实现：D-CLOT使用k均值更新原型；D-CLOT_B则利用精炼后的传输计划，将原型更新为OT加权重心，从而得到与当前传输几何一致且感知分配的原型更新。

Result: 在五个公认的动作分割基准测试上，两种方法均提升了片段级质量。在YTI数据集上，单视频F1分数和mIoU分别提升高达+12.7和+10.2；在FS-Eval数据集上，活动级F1分数提升高达+8.9。此外，本文首次在Assembly101这一比现有基准更程序化、更细粒度的数据集上，建立了无监督动作分割的基线。广泛的消融实验和敏感性分析证明，两种精炼机制是互补且鲁棒的。

Conclusion: 本文成功识别并解决了现有基于OT的无监督动作分割方法中的“表征-原型不一致”问题。通过引入图约束模块和动态原型重估计机制（特别是D-CLOT_B的OT重心更新），所提方法有效提升了分割性能，尤其是在处理动作边界和稀有动作时。研究不仅在多个标准基准上取得了显著改进，还为更复杂的细粒度任务（Assembly101）建立了新的无监督基线，证明了该方法的有效性和普适性。

Abstract: Optimal transport (OT) has emerged as an effective framework for unsupervised action segmentation. Yet, in existing OT-based methods, the latent action prototypes that define the OT costs are not re-estimated from the refined frame geometry. Instead, they evolve solely through gradients from the pseudo-label loss. We identify this \emph{representation--prototype inconsistency} as a central bottleneck, particularly around ambiguous transitions and for short or infrequent actions. To address this issue, we build on the recently introduced CLOT, which refines frame embeddings based on estimated segment embeddings, and further re-estimates the action prototypes from the refined frame embeddings. Specifically, we introduce a graph-constrained module that regularizes the OT-refined frame and segment representations by preserving the local neighborhood geometry of the encoder output. An action-embedding refinement step then periodically re-anchors the prototypes to this stabilized representation geometry. We study two instantiations that share the same backbone, graph module, and objective: D-CLOT updates the prototypes using $k$-means, whereas D-CLOT$_{B}$ updates them as OT barycenters weighted by the refined transport plan, yielding an assignment-aware prototype update consistent with the current transport geometry. Across five established benchmarks, both variants improve segment-level quality over CLOT, with per-video gains of up to $+12.7$ F1 and $+10.2$ mIoU (YTI) and activity-level gains of up to $+8.9$ F1 (FS-Eval). We further establish the first unsupervised action-segmentation baseline on Assembly101, a procedural and substantially more fine-grained benchmark than those commonly used in prior work. Extensive ablations and sensitivity analyses demonstrate that the two refinement mechanisms are complementary and robust.

</details>


### [71] [MAVISEG: Manifold Propagation and Visual Prototypes for Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Segmentation in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05878)
*Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Xujun Che,Ritabrata Chakraborty,Xi Niu,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAVISEG，一种无训练的优化层，通过挖掘文本到图像扩散Transformer模型中的结构化信号（如生成轨迹的时序信息、概念视觉外观统计和图像特征几何关系），显著提升了零样本开放词汇语义分割的性能，在六个基准测试中均达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本语义分割方法（如基于输出空间相似度或交叉注意力权重的属性方法）独立处理每个像素，忽略了扩散Transformer模型自身所蕴含的丰富结构化信息。这些信息包括生成过程的时序动态、概念的视觉外观统计以及像素特征间的几何关系，其丢失限制了分割精度。

Method: 提出MAVISEG，一个无训练的优化层。它仅以像素-概念评分场和像素特征空间作为输入，通过设计的操作来恢复和利用扩散模型中的三类结构化信号：生成轨迹的时序结构、概念视觉外观统计以及图像的特征几何关系。关键特点是“捕获不可知”，即不绑定于任何特定的初始属性计算方法。

Result: 在六个基准测试上进行评估，MAVISEG在所有无训练方法中取得了最强的整体结果，在每个基准测试上均获得了最佳的平均交并比（mIoU）。实验发现，该方法的提升在初始属性计算最弱的场景中最为显著，且不同的优化算子根据其处理的噪声场特性发挥各自的作用。

Conclusion: 扩散Transformer模型蕴含的关于概念的信息比现有属性方法所能提取的要丰富得多。MAVISEG通过恢复模型中被忽略的结构化信号，有效提升了分割性能。这表明，零样本分割精度受限的主要原因在于信息在提取和优化过程中的丢失，而非模型本身缺乏相关概念知识。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion transformers learn about objects and scenes by learning to generate them, making them strong candidates for training-free zero-shot open-vocabulary semantic segmentation. State-of-the-art attribution methods score each pixel independently, comparing its features against a fixed text-derived class representation, whether as an output-space similarity or as a cross-attention weight. This discards structured signals the model itself exposes: the temporal structure of the generative trajectory, the visual appearance statistics of each concept, and the image's own pairwise feature geometry. We present MAVISEG, a training-free refinement layer that recovers these signals. Because its operators consume only a pixel-by-concept score field and a pixel feature space, MAVISEG is capture-agnostic rather than tied to one attribution method. Across six benchmarks it achieves the strongest overall results among training-free methods, including the best mIoU on every benchmark. Interestingly, gains are largest where the initial capture is weakest, and individual operators contribute depending on the noise in the field they refine. Our results indicate that diffusion transformers carry more concept-level information than current attribution methods recover, and that much of it is lost on the way to the mask rather than absent from the model.

</details>


### [72] [To See a World in a Living Context: Unified Indoor-Outdoor Urban World Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05879)
*Xiaobin Huang,Zilong Huang,Yang Luo,Hongchao Fan,Yiping Chen,Ting Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HoloWorld是一个统一室内室外城市世界生成的框架，通过从用户描述初始化并持续更新跨尺度世界上下文，自回归生成具有几何和视觉一致性的3D城市环境。该框架在城市外部生成性能上优于现有技术，并保持了建筑级别的室内外对应和跨区块连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的3D生成在户外和室内场景方面已取得进展，但这些领域通常独立合成，缺乏室内和室外之间的对应关系，无法形成连贯的城市世界。因此，需要一种能够统一室内和室外生成的方法，以创建具有几何和视觉一致性的完整城市环境。

Method: HoloWorld框架从用户描述初始化，逐步表示和更新从城市规模规划到个体建筑的跨尺度世界上下文。它基于演化上下文和先前生成的邻近区块，自回归地生成城市外部环境，并将外部表示基于3D建筑实例和足迹，从而实现建筑特定的室内生成，具有几何约束布局和继承的外观特征。

Result: 实验表明，HoloWorld在城市外部生成性能上优于现有技术，平均AQS分数提高了7.68%，并获得最高的平均RDR分数，同时保持了统一3D城市世界中建筑级别的室内外对应和跨区块连续性。

Conclusion: HoloWorld是第一个在连贯3D城市世界中统一室内和室外生成的框架，实现了优越的城市外部生成性能和建筑级别的室内外对应性，为创建连贯的虚拟城市环境提供了新方法。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D generation has advanced rapidly in creating large-scale outdoor environments and detailed indoor scenes, but these domains are usually synthesized independently, lacking the correspondence required for a coherent urban world. We present HoloWorld, a unified indoor-outdoor urban world generation framework built on a continuously updated cross-scale world context. Initializing from a user description, HoloWorld progressively represents and updates the diverse world information, from city-scale planning to individual buildings, allowing generated interiors to maintain explicit correspondence with their associated exterior buildings. Conditioned on the evolving context and previously generated neighboring blocks, HoloWorld autoregressively generates urban exteriors with consistent spatial organization and visual identity across blocks. The generated exterior representations are further grounded in 3D building instances and footprints, enabling building-specific indoor generation with geometry-constrained layouts and inherited appearance characteristics. To our knowledge, HoloWorld is the first framework to unify indoor and outdoor generation within a coherent 3D urban world. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloWorld achieves superior urban exterior generation performance, improving the average AQS score over the SOTA by 7.68\% and obtaining the highest average RDR score, while maintaining strong building-level indoor-outdoor correspondence and cross-block continuity within a unified 3D urban world.

</details>


### [73] [Mapping Armenian Paris: Extracting and Geocoding Commercial Advertisements from the 20th-Century Diaspora Press](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05911)
*Chahan Vidal-Gorène,Seda Kirakosyan,Edita Matevosyan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于IIIF的端到端流水线，用于将数字化的法国亚美尼亚新闻转化为20世纪巴黎亚美尼亚商业社区的交互式地图。该系统通过视觉语言模型（VLMs）自动识别、阅读和解析商业广告，克服了西方亚美尼亚语资源匮乏及传统OCR在弯曲扫描页面上表现不佳的挑战。研究贡献了一个500页的标注语料库、一个Label Studio标注模板以及一个可复用的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西方亚美尼亚语是资源匮乏的语言，缺乏现成的版面分析和OCR模型。传统OCR方法（如基于CRNN的字符识别）在弯曲扫描的历史文献上性能下降。研究旨在自动化处理大量数字化的亚美尼亚历史新闻，从中提取商业广告信息，以重建和可视化20世纪巴黎亚美尼亚商业社区的地理分布。

Method: 研究构建了一个基于IIIF的端到端流水线。核心是利用视觉语言模型（VLMs）进行数据引导：首先对扫描页面进行版面分析，定位广告区域；然后对广告文本进行OCR识别；接着将识别出的文本解析为结构化记录（如商家名称、地址、业务类型）；最后对结构化记录进行地理编码，并映射到交互式地图上。研究还设计了一个Label Studio模板，以在单次标注流程中完成检测和语义字段标注。

Result: 1. 创建了一个包含500页西方亚美尼亚新闻的语料库，其中包含3,270个广告级别的精细标注。2. 设计了一个集成检测与语义标注的Label Studio模板，提高了标注效率。3. 验证了视觉语言模型（VLMs）作为数据引导策略的有效性，其在资源匮乏场景及处理弯曲扫描页面时，性能优于传统CRNN OCR方法。4. 最终生成了一个交互式地图，直观展示了20世纪巴黎亚美尼亚商业社区的商业网络与地理分布。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）驱动的数据引导是应对资源匮乏历史语言（如西方亚美尼亚语）文档数字化与解析挑战的一种有效策略。本研究建立的、以VLM为核心的工作流程具有可复用性，可为其他类似的历史文献数字化项目提供参考。研究证明，通过自动化流水线，能够大规模地将非结构化历史文献转化为结构化数据并实现空间可视化，为历史与社会研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end, IIIF-based pipeline that turns the digitised Armenian press of France into an interactive map of the 20th-century Parisian Armenian commercial community. On each page, commercial advertisements are located, read, and parsed into structured records, which are then geocoded and placed on the map. Western Armenian is under-resourced and unsupported by off-the-shelf layout and OCR models, so the pipeline uses vision-language models (VLMs) as a data-bootstrapping strategy: they produce usable structured records at a scale hand annotation could not reach, and stay reliable on the strongly curved scans where conventional line-level CRNN OCR breaks down. The contribution includes a 500-page Western Armenian press corpus with 3,270 advertisement-level annotations, a Label Studio template that captures detection and semantic fields in a single annotation pass, and a reproducible workflow transposable to other under-resourced historical corpora. More broadly, the work shows that VLM-driven data bootstrapping is an effective lever for under-resourced historical languages such as (Western) Armenian.

</details>


### [74] [Floating Radiance Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05920)
*Krzysztof Byrski,Rafał Tobiasz,Grzegorz Wilczyński,Mikołaj Zieliński,Dawid Baran,Dominik Belter,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlaRe（Floating Radiance Networks），一种结合显式可光线追踪几何与连续神经辐射场的通用神经场景表示。该方法使用浮点平面广义高斯原语，并为每个原语配备一个局部辐射场的紧凑潜在描述符，通过共享解码器映射到颜色和不透明度，从而在保持神经场表现力的同时提供显式、可寻址的结构，支持高效查询、操作以及硬件加速的交互式光线追踪渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数先进的神经场景表示方法虽然能实现逼真的新视角合成，但通常与单一的渲染范式（如基于体绘制）紧密耦合，这限制了其多功能性，并难以与传统基于光线追踪的图形工作流集成。

Method: 1. 场景表示：使用浮动平面广义高斯原语，每个原语携带一个描述局部辐射场的紧凑潜在描述符。2. 解码网络：采用一个轻量级共享解码器，以该描述符、局部表面坐标和视角方向为输入，输出颜色和不透明度。3. 渲染与操作：利用硬件加速的原语光线交叉计算，实现交互式渲染和递归光线追踪（支持反射、折射、透明和阴影）。该表示形式还支持原语级变形、网格提取以及在描述符空间内进行外观编辑。

Result: 在标准重建基准测试上的实验表明，FlaRe在使用一组紧凑原语的情况下，达到了具有竞争力的渲染质量，同时支持光线追踪和几何操作。

Conclusion: FlaRe是一种多功能的统一场景表示，它成功地将高保真神经渲染、光线追踪、几何操作和外观编辑能力整合到一个模型中。

Abstract: Recent advances in neural scene representations enable photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet most methods remain tightly coupled to a single rendering paradigm, limiting their versatility and integration with conventional graphics workflows. We introduce Floating Radiance Networks (FlaRe), a neural scene representation combining explicit ray-traceable geometry with continuous neural radiance functions. A scene is represented by floating planar generalized Gaussian primitives, each carrying a compact latent descriptor of a local radiance field. A lightweight decoder shared across the scene maps this descriptor, local surface coordinates, and viewing direction to color and opacity. This formulation preserves the expressiveness of neural fields while providing an explicitly addressable structure that can be efficiently queried and manipulated. Hardware-accelerated primitive intersections enable interactive rendering and recursive ray-tracing, including reflections, refractions, transparency, and shadows. The same representation further supports primitive-level deformation, mesh extraction, and appearance stylization directly in its learned descriptor space. Experiments across standard reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate competitive rendering quality while using a compact set of primitives. Together, these results establish FlaRe as a versatile representation that brings high-fidelity neural rendering, ray-tracing, geometric manipulation, and appearance editing into a unified scene model. Source code is available online. Source code can be found at: https://github.com/KByrski/FlaRe

</details>


### [75] [MirrorNet: Can Medical Image Anonymization Really Protect Patient Identity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05938)
*Attila Simkó*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 医学影像即使经过元数据去除，其像素内容本身仍可能识别患者身份，因为医学图像本质上是患者的一种"照片"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学影像的共享实践假设去除元数据即可匿名化，但忽略了图像内容本身可能包含可识别患者身份的信息。

Method: 使用一对耦合的、循环一致的变分自编码器，学习横断面医学图像与非医学的、可识别患者的图像之间的跨域对应关系。

Result: 从去标识化的扫描中重建了患者可识别的肖像（身份区域平均绝对误差为0.163）；反之，也能从此类图像合成出扫描。

Conclusion: 去标识化的医学扫描仍然是可识别的，本质上可视为患者的"照片"；医学影像数据应被视为生物识别数据，而非可匿名化的记录，并需要相应治理。

Abstract: Medical images are routinely de-identified---names, dates, and other metadata removed---and then shared for research, teaching, and public benchmarks under the assumption that this renders them anonymous. Such de-identification protects the metadata but not the pixels, and---apart from scans that directly contain facial structures---whether the image content itself identifies the patient has received little scrutiny. We investigate this question by learning a cycle-consistent correspondence between a cross-sectional medical image and a non-medical, patient-identifying image, using a pair of coupled, cycle-consistent variational autoencoders. From a held-out scan, the model recovers a recognisable likeness of the patient (identity-region MAE = 0.163); conversely, it synthesises a scan from such an image. These results indicate that a de-identified medical scan remains identifying---it is, in effect, a photograph of the patient---and that imaging data should be governed as biometric data rather than as anonymisable records. To support reproducibility, the code and trained models are shared at https://github.com/attilasimko/public-repository.

</details>


### [76] [Respect Your Zero-Shot Uncertainty: Conservative Calibration for Test-Time-Adapted Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05945)
*Jingyan Jiang,Yaru Sun,Xiao Chen,Jiazhen Huang,Caiting Li,Zhijian He,Yin Chen,Pingting Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现测试时自适应会降低视觉-语言模型的校准性，并提出了名为ZAEC的无标签后校准方法，该方法利用零样本熵作为参考，通过最小温度缩放恢复熵值，从而有效提升模型的校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应虽然能提升模型在分布偏移下的识别准确率，但往往会导致校准性下降，使得预测置信度不可靠。现有的无标签校准方法存在局限性，或与提示优化耦合，或依赖于对预测分布刻画粗糙的逻辑值范围统计量。论文发现TTA中存在一种称为“预测保持锐化”的现象：即使在Top-1预测及其正确性不变的情况下，模型的置信度也会增加，熵值也会降低。因此，需要一种更精细、独立且有效的校准方法。

Method: 基于对“预测保持锐化”现象及熵减少与校准误差增加关联性的分析，本文提出了一种无标签、无参数的后校准方法——零样本锚定熵校准。该方法的核心是将零样本预测的熵作为每个样本特定的不确定性参考标准。对于经过TTA后熵值降低（即被锐化）的预测，ZAEC通过最小化的温度缩放来选择性恢复其熵值至接近零样本水平，而对其他预测保持不变。这种方法不改变类别的排序和分类准确率。

Result: 在多种TTA方法和多个基准数据集上的实验表明，与TTA后相比，熵减少程度越大的样本，其预期校准误差增加越明显。在熵减少的样本上，置信度的提升往往超过准确率的提升。所提出的ZAEC方法表现优异，在ViT-B/16模型上，在五种TTA方法和15个数据集上实现了最低的后校准宏平均ECE，在RN50模型上也取得了一致的改进效果。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了测试时自适应中“预测保持锐化”这一损害校准性的关键机制，并基于此提出了一种简单而有效的ZAEC后校准方法。ZAEC无需标签数据或额外参数，能精准地对因TTA而过度自信的预测进行校准，显著改善模型在各种场景下的可靠性，为提升TTA在实际决策应用中的可信度提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) can improve the recognition accuracy of vision-language models under distribution shift, but often degrades calibration, making predictive confidence unreliable for downstream decision-making. Many existing label-free calibration approaches are either coupled to prompt optimization or rely on logit-range statistics that provide only a coarse characterization of the predictive distribution. We show that TTA can increase confidence and reduce entropy even when the top-1 prediction and its correctness remain unchanged, a failure mode we term prediction-preserving sharpening. Across diverse TTA methods and benchmarks, larger entropy reductions relative to paired zero-shot predictions are associated with greater increases in Expected Calibration Error (ECE). On entropy-reduced samples, confidence gains also tend to exceed accuracy gains. Based on these findings, we propose Zero-Shot-Anchored Entropy Calibration (ZAEC), a label-free post-hoc method that uses zero-shot entropy as a sample-specific uncertainty reference. ZAEC selectively restores the zero-shot entropy of sharpened predictions through minimal temperature scaling while leaving all other predictions unchanged. It requires no labeled calibration data or learned parameters and preserves class rankings and classification accuracy. Across five TTA methods and 15 datasets, ZAEC achieves the lowest post-hoc macro-average ECE on ViT-B/16, with consistent gains on RN50.

</details>


### [77] [Big, Bright, or Invisible: A Frozen-Feature Benchmark of 3D CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05960)
*Maulik Chevli,Johannes Brandt,Rickmer Braren,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究评估了3D CT基础模型在胸部CT扫描中的诊断广度，发现模型对广泛或高对比度异常（如设备和积液）检测效果好，但对小的低对比度病变仍面临挑战，性能主要由物理对比度和病变大小决定，而非模型架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规CT解读全面捕捉偶然发现，3D CT基础模型可通过提供解剖和病理的可泛化表征来辅助该过程。本研究旨在评估这些模型的诊断广度，以了解其在临床场景中的应用潜力。

Method: 研究基准测试了十个冻结的CT编码器，使用三种评估方法（k近邻、零样本提示、线性探测），在三个胸部CT扫描队列上进行评估，包括一个未见过的内部临床数据集。

Result: 研究发现：1) 没有通用的最先进模型，排名随评估上下文显著波动；2) 结合精细图像分词和视觉-语言对齐的模型通常表现最佳；3) 轻量级监督编码器仍具有高度竞争力，表明显式标签可替代模型规模；4) 性能的主要决定因素是物理瓶颈，即发现的可检测性与其对比度及空间范围相关；5) 广泛或高对比度异常（如设备、积液）可被可靠检测，而小的低对比度局灶性病变在所有编码器中仍是持续挑战。

Conclusion: 小的低对比度病变检测困难源于全局池化嵌入的固有限制。要准确表示这类结构，需要采用区域或病变级别的预训练方法，而非依赖全局特征。

Abstract: Routine CT interpretation is inherently comprehensive, capturing incidental findings across the entire scan volume. 3D CT foundation models could assist this process by providing generalizable representations of anatomy and pathology. To evaluate their diagnostic breadth, we benchmark ten frozen CT encoders across three cohorts of thoracic CT scans, including an unseen internal clinical dataset, using $k$-nearest neighbors, zero-shot prompting, and linear probing. We find no universal state-of-the-art, with rankings fluctuating significantly depending on the evaluation context. While models combining fine-grained image tokenization with vision-language alignment generally perform best, a lightweight supervised encoder remains highly competitive, demonstrating that explicit labels can effectively substitute for scale. Crucially, rather than model architecture, we observe that the primary determinant of performance is a physical bottleneck: a finding's detectability scales with its contrast against surrounding tissue and its spatial extent. Through controlled within-organ comparisons, we empirically demonstrate that widespread or high-contrast abnormalities, such as devices and effusions, are reliably recovered. Conversely, small, low-contrast focal lesions remain a persistent challenge across all evaluated encoders. We attribute this to the inherent limitations of globally pooled embeddings, suggesting that accurately representing small, low-contrast structures will require region- or lesion-level pretraining.

</details>


### [78] [Topology-Aware Neighborhood Learning for Source-Free Cross-Scene Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05964)
*Qingmei Li,Juepeng Zheng,Jiarui Zhang,Jianxi Huang,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Domain adaptation has advanced cross-scene hyperspectral image classification, significantly improving discriminative capability in complex scenarios. However, privacy rules or storage limits often block access to data from the source domain. Conventional domain adaptation methods become impractical, severely restricting their utility in realistic remote sensing scenarios. To tackle this challenge, we propose a topology-aware source-free learning framework. We first introduce the entropy momentum pseudo-labeling (EMP) to refine k-means assignments by leveraging entropy-aware confidence and temporal prediction momentum. Under the guidance of the refined pseudo-labels, we further utilize the contextual neighborhood topology (CNT) to exploit the intrinsic geometric structure of the target feature space. Combining the global structural information extracted by collaborative representation with the local similarity information modeled by nearest neighbor search, the CNT accomplishes the comprehensive encoding of manifold-level geometric properties in the target domain feature space. The overall objective integrates cross-entropy on refined pseudo-labels, log inner product-based topology consistency, and an information-maximization term for balanced classification, ensuring stable adaptation in the source-free setting. Extensive experiments on three typical cross-scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method exceeds state-of-the-art performance, and ablation studies further validate the contribution of each module. The results highlight the critical role of topology-aware modeling in achieving robust and accurate classification without source data.

</details>


### [79] [Diff-VF: Training-free High-quality Long Video Generation via Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05976)
*Haoning Yang,Xinyuan Chen,Yaohui Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-VF，一个无需训练、即插即用、与模型无关的框架，可将现有的短视频扩散模型转换为长视频生成器，无需修改基础模型。它通过混合噪声初始化、加权窗口采样和时间扩展采样等技术，在生成长视频时有效平衡了时间连贯性和运动多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频扩散模型大多使用短视频训练，在生成长视频时性能下降，难以在保持多样运动的同时维持长程时间连贯性。

Method: 提出Diff-VF框架，包含三种核心策略：1. 混合噪声初始化（HNI）约束全局语义；2. 加权窗口采样（WWS）消除窗口间不连续性；3. 时间扩展采样（TES）通过时变融合建立长程依赖。此外，通过跳跃残差指导进行长视频增强，以平衡保真度和真实感。

Result: 在VBench-Long基准上的评估显示，Diff-VF在时间连贯性和运动多样性之间实现了优于基础模型及FreeNoise、FreeLong、RIFLEx等现有无需训练方法的平衡，同时保持了有竞争力的逐帧质量。在两个基础模型上的实验验证了其对不同时空建模策略的适用性。消融研究证实了各组件和超参数的有效性。

Conclusion: Diff-VF是一种有效的无需训练框架，能够将短视频扩散模型扩展为高质量的长视频生成器，在保证时间连贯性的同时促进多样运动，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Recently, diffusion models have made great progress in video generation. However, most existing video diffusion models are trained with short videos, and degrade when extrapolated to long videos, struggling to maintain long-range temporal coherence while retaining diverse motions. To generate consistent, high-quality and dynamic long videos, we propose Diff-VF, a training-free, plug-and-play and model-agnostic framework that converts existing short-video diffusion backbones into long-video generators without modifying or fine-tuning the base model. Diff-VF couples three complementary strategies: Hybrid Noise Initialization (HNI) to constrain global semantics, Weighted Window Sampling (WWS) to remove inter-window discontinuities, and Temporal Extended Sampling (TES) to establish long-range dependencies with a timestep-varying fusion. We further extend Diff-VF to long-video enhancement via Skip Residual Guidance that balances fidelity and realism through timestep-dependent guidance. VBench-Long evaluation results show that Diff-VF achieves a more favorable balance between temporal coherence and motion diversity than base models and recent training-free long video generation baselines, including FreeNoise, FreeLong, and RIFLEx, while maintaining competitive frame-wise quality. Experiments on two base models demonstrate the applicability to video diffusion models with different spatial-temporal modeling strategies. Extensive ablations validate the contribution of each component and hyperparameters.

</details>


### [80] [Multi-Year Geospatial Reasoning using Interannually-Consistent Historical Predictions as a Free Input Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05979)
*Syed Roshaan Ali Shah,Kasper Bonte,David Bekaert,Kristof Van Tricht,Dieter Wens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将历史预测和外部数据（如BVL掩码）整合到深度学习模型中的方法，用于提高欧洲作物类型制图的准确性。通过引入CTY嵌入编码器和一致性地使用BVL掩码，模型能够在多年数据上进行推理，从而改善了分类性能，特别是在多年生和树木作物上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前，用于生成地球观测产品（如作物类型图）的机器学习模型通常每年独立运行，其产生的历史预测数据和外部辅助数据很少被反馈回模型内部。这些信号通常仅用于模型外部的后处理或作为固定输入掩码。研究的动机在于探索如何将这些累积的有用信号（历史预测和外部数据）有效整合到模型中，从而提升模型的推理能力和分类精度，使系统能够“跨年推理”。

Method: 研究以哥白尼土地监测服务的高分辨率作物层为测试平台。主要方法包括：1. 引入作物类型嵌入编码器，将每一份历史预测表示为一个基于置信度缩放、按时间排序的分类标记，并在年份轴上进行注意力处理。2. 研究如何将外部提供的基础植被层掩码以一致的方式整合到模型的输入和输出中。3. 为在重新标记非作物像素时公平比较不同设计，仅对18个作物类别进行评估，并分别报告精确率和召回率。

Result: 在包含约540万个标记像素的泛欧洲数据集上，实验结果表明：1. 添加预测历史记录后，仅作物类别的F1分数提高了1.6个百分点。2. 更重要的是，它修正了偏向召回率的误差分布，对多年生和树木作物（如橄榄、水果、坚果）的改进最大（分别提高了4.6、3.7和3.2个百分点）。3. 在历史记录和目标年份中一致地表示BVL掩码，为作物类别额外带来了约2.5个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种低成本、可复用的方法，用于提升任何定期生成分类地图的地球观测或基础模型的性能。通过将模型自身的历史预测和外部数据有效整合到模型内部，可以实现跨年度的推理，显著提高作物类型制图的准确性，尤其是对于特定类别。这为操作性地球观测系统的模型改进提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Machine learning, and deep networks in particular, are increasingly used to derive higher-level Earth observation (EO) products such as annual land-cover and crop-type maps. Many are generated operationally: each year a new acquisition is processed, typically with the same model, extending a multi-year archive. In the process these systems accumulate two kinds of useful signal that are almost never fed back into the model: the system's own archive of past predictions, and ancillary layers produced by other partners in a processing consortium. Both are normally used outside the network, as rule-based post-processing or a fixed input mask. Using the Copernicus Land Monitoring Service High Resolution Layer (HRL) Croplands crop-type product as a testbed, we show that bringing both signals inside the model turns a single-year, single-task pixel classifier into one that reasons across years. We introduce a Crop Type (CTY) embedding encoder that represents each past prediction as a confidence-scaled, time-ordered categorical token and attends over the year axis, and we study how the externally provided Base Vegetation Layer (BVL) mask should be represented in the model's inputs and outputs. To compare designs fairly when they relabel non-crop pixels, we evaluate on the 18 crop classes only and report precision and recall separately. On a pan-European dataset of about 5.4M labelled pixels, adding the prediction history raises crop-only F1 by 1.6 percentage points (pp) and, more importantly, corrects a recall-skewed error profile, with the largest gains on perennial and tree crops (olives +4.6, fruits +3.7, nuts +3.2 pp). Representing the BVL mask consistently in both the history and the target year adds about 2.5 pp on the crop classes. The approach is a low-cost recipe for any recurring geospatial or foundation model that emits class maps.

</details>


### [81] [Universal Concept Disruption for SAM3 Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05983)
*Hao Wang,Yuxuan Zhang,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对SAM3开放词汇概念分割模型的通用对抗攻击方法UCD，该方法通过学习单一图像扰动，在多个数据集上显著降低了分割性能，并展现出良好的迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM3模型从几何驱动的掩码预测扩展到了文本引导的开放词汇概念分割，其存在门控设计虽然提升了概念级别的预测能力，但这种集成系统在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性尚未被探索，因此有必要研究其对抗脆弱性。

Method: 提出了通用概念扰乱攻击（UCD）。该方法从（图像，名词短语）对中学习一个有界的图像扰动，作为一个统一的对抗样本。它同时破坏模型的文本条件输入路径，最大化提示共享的视觉特征的发散性，抑制最终的存在门控概念分数，并通过区域收缩和干净掩码的Dice系数破坏来损害保留掩码的空间有效性。

Result: 在SACo-Gold、LVIS、RefCOCO、PhraseCut和OpenImages数据集上进行实验，UCD在统一的评估协议下始终优于所有基线方法。具体而言，它将平均掩码AP从59.43大幅降低至18.73，将平均概念引导F1分数（cgF1）从50.32降低至20.49。此外，学习到的扰动无需重新优化即可迁移到SAM3.1模型和SAM3的视频推理任务中，而现有的防御手段（如提示集成、轻量级头部微调和时间过滤）只能带来有限的性能恢复。

Conclusion: UCD是首个针对SAM3开放词汇概念分割系统的通用跨概念对抗攻击。研究表明SAM3在对抗攻击下存在显著脆弱性，且这种脆弱性可以跨模型版本和任务模式迁移。现有的防御策略效果有限，凸显了开发更强大防御机制的必要性。

Abstract: SAM3 extends promptable segmentation from geometry-driven mask prediction to open-vocabulary concept segmentation, where a text-conditioned grounding model decides whether a concept is present and segments all matching instances. While this presence-gated design improves concept-level prediction, its adversarial robustness remains unexplored. In this paper, we introduce Universal Concept Disruption (UCD), the first universal cross-concept adversarial attack tailored to SAM3 image segmentation. UCD learns a single bounded image perturbation from (image, noun-phrase) pairs and attacks SAM3 as an integrated concept-grounding system. It jointly disrupts the text-conditioned input path, maximizes divergence in prompt-shared visual features, suppresses the final presence-gated concept scores, and corrupts the spatial validity of retained masks through area collapse and clean-mask Dice disruption. Across SACo-Gold, LVIS, RefCOCO, PhraseCut, and OpenImages datasets, UCD consistently outperforms all baselines under a matched evaluation protocol, reducing average mask AP from 59.43 to 18.73 and average cgF1 from 50.32 to 20.49. The learned perturbation also transfers to SAM3.1 and to SAM3 video inference without re-optimization, while prompt ensembling, lightweight head fine-tuning, and temporal filtering provide limited recovery.

</details>


### [82] [Wan-Animate-2: Pushing the Application Boundaries of Character Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06009)
*Guangyuan Wang,Li Hu,Dechao Meng,Zhongyi Zhang,Peng Zhang,Mingyang Huang,Ruoshi Zhang,Ke Sun,Zhe Zhang,Xingjun Wang,Gang Cheng,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Wan-Animate-2，一个端到端的角色动画框架，直接在重新设计的扩散Transformer中处理驱动视频，消除了中间运动提取器，并通过其高效变体Wan-Animate-2-Lite实现了实时推理，为数字头像等交互式应用提供了新的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色动画方法存在三大局限：基于显式运动表示的方法易出现提取误差和身份漂移；基于隐式运动特征的方法会丢失细粒度动态；基于上下文学习的方法计算成本过高。此外，所有现有系统均为离线合成设计，无法满足数字头像、直播主播等交互式应用对实时性的需求。

Method: 提出端到端的Wan-Animate-2框架，其核心是一个重新设计的扩散Transformer，直接以驱动视频为输入，完全摒弃中间运动提取器，从而实现优越的运动保真度和身份保持。引入了文本驱动的视角控制，将输出摄像机视角与驱动视频解耦。为降低延迟，提出高效变体Wan-Animate-2-Lite，采用三阶段训练范式：1）带错误缓冲机制的教师强制预训练；2）基于分块反向传播的自我强制蒸馏。

Result: 定性和用户研究表明，Wan-Animate-2在各种角色和运动模式下均能实现高保真动画效果。其高效变体Wan-Animate-2-Lite将推理延迟降至实时阈值，使流式角色动画成为可能，开辟了此前不可行的交互式应用部署场景。

Conclusion: Wan-Animate-2通过消除中间运动表示、引入视角解耦控制并推出实时高效变体，成功解决了角色动画领域的核心挑战，在生成质量与推理效率上均取得突破，为交互式虚拟角色应用奠定了基础。作者将公开发布基础模型权重以促进社区研究。

Abstract: Character image animation remains a foundational yet challenging task in computer vision. Existing approaches can be broadly categorized into three paradigms: methods based on explicit motion representations suffer from extraction errors and identity drift; methods based on implicit motion features lose fine-grained dynamics through compression; and in-context learning approaches avoid intermediate representations but incur prohibitive computational costs. Furthermore, all current systems are designed for offline synthesis, unable to meet the real-time requirements of interactive applications such as digital avatars and live-streaming hosts. To address these limitations, we present Wan-Animate-2, an end-to-end character animation framework that directly consumes the driving video within a redesigned Diffusion Transformer. Our architecture achieves superior motion fidelity and identity preservation by eliminating intermediate motion extractors entirely. We further introduce text driven viewpoint control that decouples the output camera perspective from the driving video--a capability rarely supported by prior character animation methods that rely on explicit motion representations. Beyond generation quality, we present Wan-Animate-2-Lite, an efficient variant that reduces inference latency to real-time thresholds through a three-stage training paradigm: teacher forcing pretraining with error buffer mechanism, and Self-Forcing distillation with chunk-wise backpropagation. This enables streaming character animation for interactive applications, opening new deployment scenarios that were previously infeasible. Qualitative evaluations and user studies demonstrate that Wan-Animate-2 achieves high-fidelity animation results across diverse characters and motion patterns. To foster further research and community development, we will release the Wan-Animate-2-Base model weights to the public.

</details>


### [83] [Iterate or Widen? When Test-Time Refinement Helps LiDAR Scene Completion: A Controlled Study of Evidence Geometry, Training Coverage, and Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06014)
*Shijie Hao,Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Should a completion model spend extra test-time compute by iterating, or spend a similar parameter budget on a wider one-shot predictor? The answer is easily confounded by denoising curricula, corruption augmentation, capacity, and unpaired evaluation. We study this question in LiDAR semantic scene completion by comparing a one-shot predictor, a parameter-matched wider predictor, and a weight-tied multigrid refiner initialized from the same frozen predictor. The protocol separates coherent region removal, independent thinning, range-dependent attenuation, and additive clutter while preserving exact scene-condition pairing.
